Miocic vs. Ngannou 2]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC on ESPN: Brunson vs. Holland''''' (also known as '''''UFC on ESPN 21''''' and '''''UFC Vegas 22''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on March 20, 2021 at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/02/ufc-news-nevada-approves-ufc-259-ufc-260-march-las-vegas|title=Nevada Athletic Commission approves four UFC events for March in Las Vegas|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2021-02-09|access-date=2021-02-09}}\n\n==Background==\nA middleweight bout between [[Derek Brunson]] and [[Kevin Holland]] served as the event headliner.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/30619038/kevin-holland-derek-brunson-headline-ufc-fight-night-march|title=Kevin Holland-Derek Brunson to headline UFC Fight Night in March|publisher=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2020-12-29|access-date=2020-12-31}}\n\nA women's bantamweight bout between [[Marion Reneau]] and [[The Ultimate Fighter: Heavy Hitters]] women's featherweight winner [[Macy Chiasson]] was originally scheduled for [[UFC Fight Night: Overeem vs. Volkov]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/11/marion-reneau-vs-macy-chiasson-ufc-fight-night-february-6-women-mma-booking|title=Marion Reneau vs. Macy Chiasson set for UFC Fight Night on Feb. 6|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Matt Erickson and John Morgan|date=2020-11-13|access-date=2021-02-03}} However, during the week leading up to the fight, Reneau was pulled from the card after [[COVID-19 testing|testing positive for COVID-19]]. The bout was expected to remain intact and was rescheduled for [[UFC Fight Night: Rozenstruik vs. Gane]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/02/marion-reneau-vs-macy-chiasson-postponed-ufc-fight-night-184-coronavirus-mma|title=Marion Reneau positive for COVID-19, bout vs. Macy Chiasson pushed back three weeks|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2021-02-03|access-date=2021-02-03}} They were once again rescheduled on February 20, due to Chiasson suffering an injury, with the pairing taking place at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2021/02/20/chiasson-reneau-moved-again-this-time-to-march-20th-card/|title=UFC: Chiasson vs. Reneau moved again, this time to March 20 card|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Daniel Vreeland|date=2021-02-20|access-date=2021-02-21}}\n\nA light heavyweight bout between [[Paul Craig]] and [[Jamahal Hill]] was expected to place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/01/ufc-paul-craig-vs-jamahal-hill-march-20-mma-fight-booking|title=UFC adds Paul Craig vs. Jamahal Hill to March 20 lineup|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2021-01-15|access-date=2021-03-10}} However on March 10, Hill withdrew from the bout after testing positive for COVID-19. Promotion officials have not confirmed, but the pairing is expected to remain intact and be rescheduled for a future event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/03/ufc-on-espn-21-paul-craig-confirms-jamahal-hill-bout-canceled|title=UFC on ESPN 21 co-main event scrapped after Jamahal Hill forced to withdraw from Paul Craig bout|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farrah Hannoun|date=2021-03-10|access-date=2021-03-10}}\n\nFormer [[Konfrontacja Sztuk Walki#Welterweight championship|KSW Welterweight Champion]] [[Dricus du Plessis]] was expected to face [[Trevin Giles]] in a middleweight bout at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/trevin-giles-vs-dricus-du-plessis-toegevoegd-aan-ufc-evenement-op-20-maart/|title=Trevin Giles vs. Dricus Du Plessis added to UFC event on March 20|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Staff|date=2021-01-13|access-date=2021-01-13}} {{in lang|nl}} However, du Plessis pulled out due to [[travel visa|visa]] issues and was replaced by [[Roman Dolidze]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/3/10/22323869/dricus-du-plessis-out-of-ufc-vegas-22-roman-dolidze-steps-in-to-fight-trevin-giles|title=Dricus Du Plessis out of UFC Vegas 22, Roman Dolidze steps in to fight Trevin Giles|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee and Mike Heck|date=2021-03-10|access-date=2021-03-10}}\n\nA women's strawweight bout between [[Kay Hansen]] and promotional newcomer [[Cheyanne Vlismas]] was scheduled for the event.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2020/12/31/kay-hansen-vs-cheyanne-buys-added-ufcs-march-20-card/|title=Kay Hansen vs. Cheyanne Buys Added to UFC’s March 20 Card|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Jay Anderson|date=2020-12-31|access-date=2021-03-12}} However, Hansen pulled out due to undisclosed reasons and was replaced on March 12 by promotional newcomer [[Montserrat Ruiz]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/3/12/22327493/with-kay-hansen-out-cheyanne-buys-now-meets-fellow-newcomer-montserrat-ruiz-at-ufc-vegas-22|title=With Kay Hansen out, Cheyanne Buys now meets fellow newcomer Montserrat Ruiz at UFC Vegas 22|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck and Damon Martin|date=2021-03-12|access-date=2021-03-12}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between [[Johnny Eduardo]] and [[Anthony Birchak]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url= https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/02/ufc-news-johnny-eduardo-vs-anthony-birchak-booking-march-20-fight-announcement|title= Johnny Eduardo returns from 33-month layoff, fights Anthony Birchak at March 20 UFC event|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and Farrah Hannoun|date=2021-02-18|access-date=2021-03-15}} However on March 15, Eduardo withdrew from the bout due to visa issues and the bout was scrapped.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/3/15/22332169/visa-issues-johnny-eduardo-out-ufc-vegas-22-fight-anthony-birchak|title=Johnny Eduardo out of UFC Vegas 22 fight with Anthony Birchak|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz and Mike Heck|date=2021-03-15|access-date=2021-03-15}}\n\n[[Don'Tale Mayes]] was expected to face [[Tai Tuivasa]] in a heavyweight bout at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/01/ufc-fight-night-tai-tuivasa-vs-dontale-mayes-march-20|title=Tai Tuivasa vs. Don'Tale Mayes added to UFC Fight Night on March 20|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun and Nolan King|date=2021-01-20|access-date=2021-03-17}} However, Mayes was removed from the fight during the week leading up to the event for undisclosed reasons and was replaced by promotional newcomer Harry Hunsucker.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/03/tai-tuivasa-gets-harry-hunsucker-as-new-opponent-for-ufc-on-espn-21|title=Tai Tuivasa gets Harry Hunsucker as new opponent for UFC on ESPN 21|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and John Morgan|date=2021-03-17|access-date=2021-03-17}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, Jesse Strader weighed in at 137.5 pounds, one and a half pounds over the bantamweight non-title fight limit. His bout proceeded at a catchweight and he was fined 20% of his individual purse, which went to his opponent [[Montel Jackson]].{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/03/ufc-espn-21-derek-brunson-kevin-holland-weigh-in-results-live-video-stream|title=UFC on ESPN 21 weigh-in results: Stoliarenko falls on stage, bout scratched|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2021-03-19|access-date=2021-03-19}} Meanwhile, former [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Bantamweight Championship|Invicta FC Bantamweight Champion]] [[Julija Stoliarenko]] made the weight for her bout against [[Julia Avila]], but the contest was cancelled due to health concerns as Stoliarenko collapsed twice off the scales during her attempts.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/03/ufc-on-espn-21-julija-stoliarenko-collapses-twice-scary-video|title=Julija Stoliarenko collapses twice in scary scene at UFC on ESPN 21 weigh-ins, stretchered out|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn and John Morgan|date=2021-03-19|access-date=2021-03-19}}\n\nA lightweight bout between [[Gregor Gillespie]] and [[Brad Riddell]] was expected to take serve as the co-main event.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/2/19/22291667/gregor-gillespie-vs-brad-riddell-added-to-ufc-event-on-march-20|title=Gregor Gillespie vs. Brad Riddell added to UFC event on March 20|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck and Damon Martin|date=2021-02-19|access-date=2021-03-20}} However, it was cancelled on the day of the event due to COVID-19 protocols.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/03/ufc-on-espn-21-news-gregor-gillespie-brad-riddell-off-card-covid-19-protocols|title=COVID-19 protocols cause Gregor Gillespie vs. Brad Riddell scratch from UFC on ESPN 21|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2021-03-20|access-date=2021-03-20}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent [[UFC on ESPN: Chiesa vs. Magny]]\n|followingevent= [[UFC Fight Night: Overeem vs. Volkov]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC 257: Poirier vs. McGregor 2''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on January 24, 2021 at the [[Etihad Arena]] on [[Yas Island]], [[Abu Dhabi]], United Arab Emirates.{{Cite web|url=https://www.ufc.com/news/conor-mcgregor-vs-dustin-poirier-made-official-ufc-257|title=Dustin Poirier vs Conor McGregor to headline UFC 257|publisher=ufc.com|author=Staff|date=2020-11-23|access-date=2020-11-23}}{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/12/22/22195150/dana-white-announces-ufc-257-first-three-events-of-2021-to-take-place-on-fight-island|title=Dana White announces UFC 257, first three events of 2021 to take place on Fight Island|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2020-12-22|access-date=2020-12-22}}\n\n==Background==\nStarting with [[UFC on ABC: Holloway vs. Kattar]], a limited number of fans were allowed inside the newly built [[Etihad Arena]], marking the first time since [[UFC 248]] on March 7, 2020, that non-essential event personnel were in attendance.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/01/ufc-limited-fans-attendance-etihad-arena-fight-island-mma-conor-mcgregor-dustin-poirier|title=Fans are back: UFC will have 'limited' attendance at three-card 'Fight Island' stint at Etihad Arena|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2021-01-07|access-date=2021-01-07}} The venue has maximum capacity of over 18,000, but the UFC was expecting to have closer to 2,000 fans for each of the Fight Island's events during the week.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/1/10/22223333/etihad-arena-lists-official-covid-19-safety-measures-for-fans-at-january-ufc-fight-island-shows|title=Etihad Arena lists official COVID-19 safety measures for fans at January UFC Fight Island shows|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Newswire|date=2021-01-10|access-date=2021-01-11}}\n\nFor the event to be broadcast live during [[Prime time]] hours on the [[Eastern Time Zone|east coast]] of North America, the main card began at 7:00 am (January 24) local time in Abu Dhabi, with a full preliminary card beginning at approximately 4:00 am [[Gulf Standard Time]].{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/commentary-teams-broadcast-plans-set-for-three-event-stretch-on-fight-island-ending-with-ufc-257|title='Fight Island' commentary teams, broadcast plans set for three events ending with UFC 257|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2021-01-13|access-date=2021-01-13}}\n\nA lightweight rematch between former [[UFC Featherweight Championship|UFC Featherweight]] and [[UFC Lightweight Championship|Lightweight Champion]] [[Conor McGregor]] and former interim champion [[Dustin Poirier]] headlined this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/11/10/21558071/conor-mcgregor-vs-dustin-poirier-2-officially-booked-for-ufc-257|title=Conor McGregor vs. Dustin Poirier 2 in works for UFC 257|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2020-11-10|access-date=2020-11-11}}{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/11/conor-mcgregor-vs-dustin-poirier-rematch-booked-ufc-257|title=Conor McGregor vs. Dustin Poirier 2 booked for UFC 257|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Danny Segura and Mike Bohn|date=2020-11-19|access-date=2020-11-19}} They met previously in a featherweight bout at [[UFC 178]] on September 27, 2014, where McGregor won by first-round TKO.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2014/09/ufc-178-results-photos-conor-mcgregor-stops-dustin-poirier-in-first-round|title=UFC 178 results, photos: Conor McGregor stops Dustin Poirier in first round|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2014-09-27|access-date=2020-11-11}}\n\nAfter defending his lightweight title at [[UFC 254]] in October, [[Khabib Nurmagomedov]] immediately announced his retirement, citing his father's death from complications related to COVID-19 in last July as the main reason behind it.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/10/ufc-254-khabib-nurmagomedov-dominates-announces-retirement-ufc-254-justin-gaethje|title=Khabib Nurmagomedov dominates Justin Gaethje, retires after UFC 254 submission win|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Dave Doyle|date=2020-10-24|access-date=2021-01-16}} Despite his announcement, the title was never officially vacated as UFC President [[Dana White]] said several times that he believed Nurmagomedov would still fight again.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/10/ufc-news-dana-white-thinks-khabib-nurmagomedov-wont-stay-retired|title=Dana White has 'a feeling' Khabib Nurmagomedov won't stay retired after talking to him|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Simon Samano|date=2020-10-29|access-date=2021-01-16}}{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/11/dana-white-door-open-khabib-nurmagomedov-return-ufc-video|title=Dana White leaves door open for Khabib Nurmagomedov to return: 'He's still the champ'|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Simon Head and Ken Hathaway|date=2020-11-01|access-date=2021-01-16}}{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/11/dana-white-insists-conor-mcgregor-vs-dustin-poirier-not-for-the-title-khabib-nurmagomedov-will-fight-again-ufc|title=Dana White insists Conor McGregor vs. Dustin Poirier won't be for title: 'Khabib's going to fight'|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun and John Morgan|date=2020-11-18|access-date=2021-01-16}} A meeting between both of them to discuss the champion's future was then expected to happen this week during the organization's stint at [[Fight Island]], eventually taking place on January 15.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/01/ufc-news-dana-white-meeting-khabib-nurmagomedov-yes-or-no-retirement|title=Dana White anticipates Khabib Nurmagomedov meeting will be five minutes: 'It's yes or no'|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and John Morgan|date=2021-01-13|access-date=2021-01-16}}{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/01/ufc-news-dana-white-khabib-nurmagomedov-meeting-video-uae-warriors-abu-dhabi|title=Video: Dana White and Khabib Nurmagomedov finally meet face to face in Abu Dhabi|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2021-01-15|access-date=2021-01-16}} The following day, White made an appearance live on [[American Broadcasting Company|ABC]] during the main card of [[UFC on ABC: Holloway vs. Kattar]] and revealed the outcome of the meeting: the Russian left the door open to a potential return, depending on how the main and co-main event fighters perform at this event, while also praising [[Charles Oliveira]]'s performance at [[UFC 256]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/01/ufc-on-abc-1-dana-white-to-reveal-khabib-nurmagomedov-decision|title=What's happening with Khabib Nurmagomedov? Lee|date=2021-01-16|access-date=2021-01-16}} Despite White's announcement, Nurmagomedov indicated in another interview that \"fighting again is not in my plans\".{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/1/20/22241353/khabib-nurmagomedov-fighting-not-in-my-plans-dont-push-me-to-do-things-that-will-disappoint-mother|title=Khabib Nurmagomedov: Fighting not in my plans, don't 'push me to do things that will disappoint my mother'|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Newswire|date=2021-01-20|access-date=2021-01-20}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between promotional newcomers [[Umar Nurmagomedov]] and [[Sergey Morozov (fighter)|Sergey Morozov]] was initially scheduled to take place at [[UFC 254]], but Nurmagomedov pulled out due to an illness.{{cite web|url= Meanwhile, then undefeated Adesanya ''(right)'' aspired to become the first fighter to hold the light heavyweight and middleweight titles simultaneously.\n}}\n\nThe event was headlined by a [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] bout between then champion [[Jan Błachowicz]] (also former [[Konfrontacja Sztuk Walki#Light heavyweight championship|KSW Light Heavyweight Champion]]) and [[UFC Middleweight Championship|UFC Middleweight Champion]] [[Israel Adesanya]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/12/ufc-259-jan-blachowicz-israel-adesanya-light-heavyweight-title-champion-vs-champion|title=Champ vs. champ: Jan Blachowicz puts up light heavyweight title vs. Israel Adesanya at UFC 259|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2020-12-30|access-date=2020-12-31}} If successful, Adesanya would become the fourth man to be champion in two divisions simultaneously and fifth fighter overall (after [[Conor McGregor]] at [[UFC 205]], [[Daniel Cormier]] at [[UFC 226]], [[Amanda Nunes]] at [[UFC 232]] and [[Henry Cejudo]] at [[UFC 238]]), as well as the eighth person overall to win a title in different divisions. This was the sixth time in UFC history that champions in different divisions fought for the same title, following [[UFC 94]], [[UFC 205]], [[UFC 226]], [[UFC 232]] and [[UFC Fight Night: Cejudo vs. Dillashaw]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/30626888/ufc-real-not-right-israel-adesanya-double-champ-move|title=UFC real or not: This is the right time for Israel Adesanya's fight.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/2/10/22276726/glover-teixeira-backup-jan-blachowicz-israel-adesanya-ufc-259|title=Glover Teixeira says he will be backup for Jan Blachowicz vs. Błachowicz weighed in at 205 pounds and expected to come in at over 220 pounds on fight night.{{Cite web |date=2021-02-17 |title=The Takedown {{!}} Will Adesanya usher in new MMA era of no weight Fighting Championship]] that took place on April 17, 2021, at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://www.cbssports.com/mma/news/robert-whittaker-vs-paulo-costa-middleweight-bout-set-to-headline-ufc-fight-night-event-in-april/|title=Robert Whittaker vs. Paulo Costa middleweight bout set to headline UFC Fight Night event in April|publisher=cbssports.com|author=Brent Brookhouse|date=2021-02-26|access-date=2021-03-12}}\n\n==Background==\nA middleweight bout between former [[UFC Middleweight Championship|UFC Middleweight Champion]] [[Robert Whittaker (fighter)|Robert Whittaker]] (also [[The Ultimate Fighter: The Smashes]] welterweight winner) and former middleweight title challenger [[Paulo Costa]] was expected to serve as the main event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.lowkickmma.com/paulo-costa-rejects-rescheduling-against-robert-whittaker-clash-still-set-for-april-17/|title=Paulo Costa rejects rescheduling against Robert Whittaker, clash still set for April 17|publisher=lowkickmma.com|author=Ross Markey|date=2021-02-15|access-date=2021-03-12}} However, Costa withdrew from the fight on March 16 due to a severe [[flu]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/3/16/22333374/paulo-costa-ill-out-ufc-fight-robert-whittaker-mma|title=Paulo Costa ill, unable to fight Robert Whittaker at April 17 UFC event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2021-03-16|access-date=2021-03-16}} He was replaced by former interim title challenger and [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Jones vs. Team Sonnen]] middleweight winner [[Kelvin Gastelum]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/31077637/kelvin-gastelum-replace-paulo-costa-fight-robert-whittaker-april-17|title=Sources: Kelvin Gastelum to replace Paulo Costa, fight Robert Whittaker on April 17|publisher=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2021-03-16|access-date=2021-03-16}} They were head coaches on [[The Ultimate Fighter: Heavy Hitters]] and Whittaker was expected to defend his title against Gastelum at [[UFC 234]] in February 2019, but pulled out hours before that event due to a collapsed bowel and an internal hernia that required emergency surgery.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sportingnews.com/au/mma/news/ufc-234-robert-whittaker-forced-off-ufc-234-with-hernia-requires-immediate-surgery/|title=UFC 234: Robert Whittaker forced off UFC 234 with hernia, requires immediate surgery|publisher=sportingnews.com|author=Brendan Bradford|date=2019-02-09|access-date=2021-03-16}}{{Dead link|date=July 2025 |bot=InternetArchiveBot |fix-attempted=yes }}\n\nA bantamweight bout between [[Tony Gravely]] and [[Nate Maness]] was scheduled to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/02/ufc-news-tony-gravely-vs-nate-maness-april-17-event|title=UFC books Tony Gravely vs. Nate Maness for April 17 event|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and Farah Hannoun |date=2019-02-18|access-date=2021-03-26}} However, Maness was removed from the bout for undisclosed reasons and replaced by [[Anthony Birchak]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/anthony-birchak-vervangt-nate-maness-tegen-tony-gravely-tijdens-ufc-evenement-op-17-april/|title=Anthony Birchak replaces Nate Maness against Tony Gravely at UFC event on April 17th|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Staff|date=2021-03-26|access-date=2021-03-26}} {{in lang|nl}}\n\nFormer [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Atomweight Championship|Invicta FC Atomweight Champion]] and [[UFC Women's Strawweight Championship]] challenger [[Jessica Penne]] and [[Hannah Goldy]] were expected to meet in a women's strawweight bout at [[UFC 260]], but Goldy [[COVID-19 testing|tested positive for COVID-19]] and the bout was postponed.{{Cite web|url= https://www.bjpenn.com/mma-news/ufc/hannah-goldy-tests-positive-for-covid-19-fight-with-jessica-penne-at-ufc-260-off/|title=Hannah Goldy tests positive for COVID-19, fight with Jessica Penne at UFC 260 off|publisher=Bjorne.com|author=Adam Martin|date=2021-03-24|access-date=2021-03-24}} They were then rescheduled for this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.ufc.com.br/news/update-ufc-260-miocic-vs-ngannou-2-volkanovski-ortega|title=Updates to UFC 260: Miocic vs. Ngannou 2|publisher=ufc.com|author=Staff|date=2019-03-26|access-date=2021-03-30}} A week before the event, Goldy pulled out once again and was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Lupita Godinez]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/04/lupita-godinez-vs-jessica-penne-ufc-espn-22-lfa-champion-new-contract|title=LFA champ Lupita Godinez signs with UFC, meets Jessica Penne on April 17|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farrah Hannoun|date=2021-04-01|access-date=2021-04-01}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between [[Parker Porter]] and [[Chase Sherman]] was scheduled to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/01/chase-sherman-vs-parker-porter-ufc-fight-night-april-17|title=Chase Sherman vs. Parker Porter added to UFC Fight Night on April 17|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farrah Hannoun|date=2021-01-30|access-date=2021-04-08}} However, Porter was removed from the bout on April 7 due to undisclosed reasons and replaced by former [[UFC Heavyweight Championship|UFC Heavyweight Champion]] [[Andrei Arlovski]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/4/7/22372820/with-parker-porter-out-andrei-arlovski-steps-in-to-face-chase-sherman-at-ufc-vegas-24|title=With Parker Porter out, Andrei Arlovski steps in to The pairing remained intact and was rescheduled for [[UFC Fight Night: Font vs. Garbrandt]]{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/4/14/22384623/ricardo-ramos-withdraws-ufc-vegas-24-positive-covid-19-test-bill-algeo|title=Ricardo Ramos withdraws from UFC Vegas 24 after positive COVID-19 test|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz and Mike Heck |date=2021-04-14|access-date=2021-04-14}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, [[Tracy Cortez]] and [[Zarah Fairn Dos Santos]] missed weight for their respective bouts. Cortez weighed in at 126.5 pounds, half a pound over the women's flyweight non-title fight limit. Her bout proceeded at a catchweight and she was fined 20% of her individual purse, which went to her opponent [[Justine Kish]].{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/04/ufc-espn-22-robert-whittaker-kelvin-gastelum-weigh-in-results-live-video-stream|title=UFC on ESPN 22 weigh-in results: Two fighters miss, one bout scrapped|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2021-04-16|access-date=2021-04-16}} Fairn weighed in at 147 pounds, eight pounds over the pre-established limit of 139 pounds (the bout was originally set for the women's bantamweight division, but was shifted to a catchweight prior to the start of the weigh-ins). Her bout against [[Josiane Nunes]] was cancelled due to the weight discrepancy, as Nunes weighed in at 136 pounds.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/04/ufc-on-espn-22-weigh-ins-video-zarah-fairn-vs-josiane-nunes-weight-miss-11-pounds|title=Zarah Fairn vs. Josiane Nunes scrapped from UFC on ESPN 22 after Klose]] was expected to take place as the co-main event.{{cite web|url=https://www.overtimeheroics.net/2021/02/25/drakkar-klose-vs-jeremy-stephens/|title=Drakkar Klose vs Jeremy Stephens added to April 17th card|publisher=overtimeheroics.com|author=Ryan Truland|date=2021-02-25|access-date=2021-04-17}} However, Klose withdrew citing injuries that he claimed were triggered by a Stephens shove at the weigh-ins and the bout was pulled from the card just hours before the event.{{cite 262|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2021-03-19|access-date=2021-03-19}}\n\n== Background ==\nA [[UFC Lightweight Championship]] bout for the vacant title between former three-time [[List of Bellator MMA champions#Lightweight World Championship|Bellator Lightweight World Champion]] [[Michael Chandler]] and [[Charles Oliveira]] headlined the event.{{cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/31090572/dana-white-khabib-nurmagomedov-officially-retired-michael-chandler-charles-oliveira-title|title=Dana White: Khabib Nurmagomedov officially retired; Michael Chandler-Charles Oliveira for title|work=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2021-03-19|access-date=2021-03-19}} Former champion [[Khabib Nurmagomedov]] announced his retirement immediately after defending his title at [[UFC 254]] last October, citing his father's death from complications related to [[COVID-19]] in July as the main reason behind it.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/10/ufc-254-khabib-nurmagomedov-dominates-announces-retirement-ufc-254-justin-gaethje|title=Khabib Nurmagomedov dominates Justin Gaethje, retires after UFC 254 submission win|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Dave Doyle|date=2020-10-24|access-date=2021-03-19}} Despite his announcement, the title was never officially vacated as UFC President [[Dana White]] said several times that he believed Nurmagomedov would still fight again. White finally conceded on March 19 as he confirmed Nurmagomedov's retirement.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/3/19/22339456/dana-white-confirms-khabib-nurmagomedov-officially-retired-no-longer-pursuing-possible-return|title=Dana White confirms Khabib Nurmagomedov officially retired, no longer pursuing possible return|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2021-03-19|access-date=2021-03-19}}\n\nA welterweight bout between [[Leon Edwards]] and former [[UFC Lightweight Championship]] challenger [[Nate Diaz]] (also [[The Ultimate Fighter 5]] lightweight winner) was expected to take place at this event. It would've marked the first five-round non-title co-main event in UFC history.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/03/ufc-262-news-nate-diaz-vs-leon-edwards-five-rounds-co-main-event-houston|title=UFC 262 books Nate Diaz vs. Leon Edwards for five-round co-main event in Houston|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and Mike Bohn|date=2021-03-30|access-date=2021-03-30}} However, Diaz pulled out due to a minor injury in early May and the bout was postponed to [[UFC 263]].{{Cite web|url= https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/5/3/22418618/leon-edwards-vs-nate-diaz-rescheduled-from-ufc-262-in-may-to-ufc-263-in-june|title= Leon Edwards vs. Nate Diaz rescheduled from UFC 262 in May to UFC 263 in June|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2021-05-03|access-date=2021-05-03}}\n\nA flyweight bout between former [[UFC Flyweight Championship]] challenger [[Alex Perez (fighter)|Alex Perez]] and [[Matt Schnell]] was scheduled for the event.{{Cite web|url=https://empiresportsmedia.com/mma/ufc-books-alex-perez-matt-schnell/|title=UFC books Alex Perez – Matt Schnell|publisher=empiresportsmedia.com|author=Daniel Cunningham|date=2021-02-22|access-date=2021-04-22}} However, Perez was forced to pull out due to undisclosed reasons. He was replaced by [[Rogério Bontorin]] and the bout took place at bantamweight.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/04/ufc-262-alex-perez-out-rogerio-bontorin-to-fight-matt-schnell|title=With Alex Perez out, Rogerio Bontorin targeted to fight Matt Schnell at UFC 262|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2021-04-21|access-date=2021-04-22}} At the weigh-ins, Bontorin weighed in at 137 pounds, one pound over the division's non-title fight limit. The bout proceeded at catchweight and he was fined 20% of his purse, which went to Schnell.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/05/ufc-262-charles-oliveira-michael-chandler-weigh-in-results-live|title=UFC 262 weigh-in results: Charles Oliveira, Michael Chandler set for vacant title|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2021-05-14|access-date=2021-05-14}}\n\n[[Joel Álvarez]] was scheduled to face [[Christos Giagos]] in a lightweight bout.{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/joel-alvarez-treft-christos-giagos-tijdens-ufc-262/|title=Joel Alvarez faces Christos Giagos at UFC 262|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Staff|date=2021-03-12|access-date=2021-05-06}} {{in lang|nl}} However, Álvarez was removed from the bout in early May due to alleged [[visa (document)|visa]] issues that restricted his travel. Giagos faced returning veteran [[Sean Soriano]] instead.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/05/ufc-262-sean-soriano-returns-after-six-years-vs-christos-giagos|title=Sean Soriano returns to UFC following six-year absence, faces Christos Giagos at UFC 262|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=John Morgan and Nolan King|date=2021-05-06|access-date=2021-05-06}}\n\nA middleweight bout between [[Jack Hermansson]] and [[Edmen Shahbazyan]] was expected to take place at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/3/20/22341466/jack-hermansson-vs-edmen-shahbazyan-booked-for-ufc-262|title=Jack Hermansson vs. Edmen Shahbazyan booked for UFC 262|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2021-03-20|access-date=2021-05-10}} However, the bout was postponed to take place one week later at [[UFC Fight Night: Font vs. Garbrandt]] due to a COVID-19 case in Hermansson's camp.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/05/ufc-262-news-jack-hermansson-vs-edmen-shabazyan-shifts-fight-night-188|title=UFC 262 loses Jack Hermansson vs. Edmen Shahbazyan; bout pushed back one week|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2021-05-10|access-date=2021-05-10}}\n\nOn May 14, it was announced that the UFC would be increasing bonuses (Fight of the Night and Performance of the Night) from $50,000 to $75,000. This increase was for this event only and was made at the request of former interim lightweight champion [[Tony Ferguson]] (also [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Lesnar vs. Team dos Santos]] welterweight winner), who received a raucous response from the audience in attendance at the UFC 262 pre-fight press conference when he made the proposal.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/05/ufc-262-dana-white-raises-bonus-money-75k|title=Dana White obliges Tony Ferguson, increases UFC 262 bonuses to $75,000|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=John Morgan and Nolan King|date=2021-05-14|access-date=2021-05-15}}\n\nSeveral hooligans fought in the stands during this event.{{cite web | url=https://www.sportskeeda.com/mma/news-watch-fans-go-crazy-fight-breaks-stands-ufc-262 | title=Watch: Fans go crazy as a fight breaks out in the stands at UFC 262 | date=16 May 2021 web|url=https://www.ufc.com/event/ufc-262|title=UFC 262: Oliveira vs. Chandler|date=5 January 2021 |publisher=Ultimate Fighting Championship|access-date=2021-03-19}}\n\n==Bonus awards==\nThe following fighters received $75,000 bonuses.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/5/16/22438435/ufc-262-bonuses-new-champ-charles-oliveira-banks-75k-for-michael-chandler-knockout|title=UFC 262 Barboza vs. Shane Burgos'''\n* '''Performance of the Night: Charles Oliveira''' and ''' Christos Giagos'''\n\n==Aftermath==\nIn June 2021, Bontorin was notified by [[USADA]] due to a potential doping violation. He tested positive for [[hydrochlorothiazide]], a banned diuretic, in an out-of-competition urine sample in May 2021.{{cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/6/15/22534696/ufc-flyweight-rogerio-bontorin-tests-positive-diuretic-mma|title=UFC flyweight Rogerio Bontorin tests positive for diuretic|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2021-06-15|access-date=2021-06-16}} Following notification of his positive test, Bontorin provided open containers of two dietary supplements he obtained from a Brazilian compounding pharmacy that he was using prior to his positive test for analysis by a WADA-accredited laboratory. Although no prohibited substances were listed on the supplement product labels, the analysis revealed the presence of HCTZ in the products. He was given a three-month suspension that began on 1 May and his win was overturned to a no contest.{{cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/10/1/22703949/rogerio-bontorin-reduced-usada-suspension-tainted-supplement-brandon-royval-january-15-ufc|title=Rogerio Bontorin gets reduced suspension for tainted supplement, returns vs. Brandon Royval at Jan. 15 UFC event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz and Mike Heck|date=2021-10-01|access-date=2022-05-02}}{{cite news|url=https://www.ufc.com/news/rogerio-bontorin-learned-the-right-lessons-ufc-fight-night-kattar-chikadze-flyweight|title=Rogério Bontorin learned the right lessons|publisher=ufc.com|author=E. Spencer Kyte|date=2022-01-12|access-date=2022-05-02}}\n\n== See also ==\n{{Portal|Martial Arts}}\n* [[List of UFC events]]\n* [[List of current UFC fighters]]\n* [[2021 in UFC]]\n\n== References [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on June 26, 2021 at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada|Enterprise]], Nevada, part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/05/ufc-nevada-athletic-commission-approves-june-fight-night-events-las-vegas|title=Nevada approves three June UFC Fight Night events in Las Vegas|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Danny Segura|date=2021-05-11|access-date=2021-05-11}}\n\n== Background ==\nA heavyweight bout between [[Ciryl Gane]] and former [[List of Bellator MMA champions#Heavyweight World Championship|Bellator Heavyweight World Champion]] [[Alexander Volkov (fighter)|Alexander Volkov]] served as the event's headliner.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/4/3/22365714/ciryl-gane-vs-alexander-volkov-booked-as-main-event-for-ufc-card-on-june-26|title=Ciryl Gane vs. Alexander Volkov booked as main event for UFC card on June 26|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2021-04-03|access-date=2021-04-03}}\n\nA middleweight bout between [[Anthony Hernandez (fighter)|Anthony Hernandez]] and [[Punahele Soriano]] was expected to take place at the event.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/03/anthony-hernandez-vs-punahele-soriano-set-for-ufc-fight-night-in-june|title=Anthony Hernandez vs. Punahele Soriano set for UFC Fight Night in June|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=John Morgan and Nolan King|date=2021-03-18|access-date=2021-03-18}} The pairing was previously scheduled to take place in May 2020 at [[UFC on ESPN: Overeem vs. Harris]], but Soriano pulled out due to undisclosed reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/04/puna-soriano-out-kevin-holland-anthony-hernandez-ufc-may-16/amp?__twitter_impression=true|title=Kevin Holland replaces Puna Soriano, meets Anthony Hernandez on UFC's May 16 card|author=Mike Bohn|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|date=2020-04-09|access-date=2021-03-18}}{{Dead link|date=January 2026 |bot=InternetArchiveBot }} In turn, Hernandez pulled out in late May due to a hand injury and was replaced by [[Brendan Allen]], with the new matchup expected to take place a month later at [[UFC Fight Night: Sandhagen vs. Dillashaw]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/5/25/22454056/with-anthony-hernandez-out-puna-soriano-now-meets-brendan-allen-at-ufc-event-on-july-24|title=With Anthony Hernandez out, Puna Soriano now meets Brendan Allen at UFC event on July 24|author=Mike Heck|publisher=mmafighting.com|date=2020-05-25|access-date=2021-05-26}}\n\nA lightweight bout between [[Yancy Medeiros]] and [[Damir Hadžović]] has been rescheduled for the second time and took place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/05/yancy-medeiros-vs-damir-hadzovic-rebooked-ufc-fight-night-june-26-mma-matchup|title=Yancy Medeiros vs. Damir Hadzovic rebooked for UFC Fight Night on June 26|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2021-05-29|access-date=2021-05-29}} The pairing was first scheduled to meet at [[UFC Fight Night: Font vs. Garbrandt]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/04/nikolas-motta-out-yancy-medeiros-faces-damir-hadzovic-at-ufc-fight-night-188|title=With Nikolas Motta out, Yancy Medeiros steps in to face Damir Hadzovic at UFC Fight Night 188|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2021-04-16|access-date=2021-04-16}} However, it was pulled from the card just hours before taking place due to health issues with Hadžović.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/05/damir-hadzovic-vs-yancy-medeiros-pulled-from-ufc-fight-night-188-lineup|title=Damir Hadzovic vs. Yancy Medeiros pulled from UFC Fight Night 188 lineup|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2021-05-22|access-date=2021-05-22}}\n\nA women's bantamweight bout between [[Julija Stoliarenko]] and [[Julia Avila]] was also rescheduled to take place at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sportas.lt/naujiena/434665/j-stoliarenko-gavo-dar-viena-sansa-susikauti-su-j-avila-kova-ivyks-jau-si-menesi|title=Stoliarenko face [[Danilo Marques]] in a light heavyweight bout at the event.{{Cite on June 14 and replaced by [[Kennedy Nzechukwu]].{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/06/ufc-fight-night-190-news-kennedy-nzechukwu-vs-danilo-marques-ed-herman-out|title=Kennedy on ESPN: Brunson vs. Holland]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Edwards vs. Muhammad''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 187''''', '''''UFC on ESPN+ 45''''' and '''''UFC Vegas 21''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on March 13, 2021 at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://www.ufc.com/news/ufc-announces-two-upcoming-main-events|title=UFC announces two upcoming main-events|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2021-01-20|access-date=2021-01-20}}{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/02/ufc-news-nevada-approves-ufc-259-ufc-260-march-las-vegas|title=Nevada Athletic Commission approves four UFC events for March in Las Vegas|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2021-02-09|access-date=2021-02-09}}\n\n== Background ==\nA welterweight bout between [[Leon Edwards]] and [[Khamzat Chimaev]] was expected to serve as the main event.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/01/ufc-dana-white-confirms-leon-edwards-khamzat-chimaev-main-event-march-13|title=Dana White confirms Leon Edwards vs. Khamzat Chimaev rebooked for March 13 main event|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Simon Head and John Morgan|date=2021-01-13|access-date=2021-01-13}} The pairing has been scheduled as headliner and cancelled on two previous occasions due to [[COVID-19]] related issues affecting both fighters. First at [[UFC Fight Night: Thompson vs. Neal]] in December 2020, and again at [[UFC on ESPN: Chiesa vs. Magny]] in January 2021.{{cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/30429656/sources-leon-edwards-khamzat-chimaev-fight-edwards-tests-positive-covid-19%3fplatform=amp|title=Sources: Leon Edwards-Khamzat Chimaev fight off after Edwards tests positive for COVID-19|work=espn.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2020-12-01|access-date=2020-12-22}}{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/12/ufc-news-covid-reason-khamzat-chimaev-withdrew-leon-edwards-fight|title=COVID-19 complications led to Khamzat Chimaev's withdrawal from Leon Edwards bout|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Danny Segura|date=2020-12-30|access-date=2020-12-30}}{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/12/leon-edwards-khamzat-chimaev-neil-mangy-michael-chiesa-ufc-fight-night-january-20|title=Leon Edwards-Khamzat Chimaev pushed back; Neil Magny-Michael Chiesa new Jan. 20 main event|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2020-12-30|access-date=2020-12-31}} On February 11, the fight was cancelled a third time as it was announced that Chimaev was still suffering from [[Long COVID|lingering effects of COVID-19]] and pulled out of the fight.{{cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/30882760/khamzat-chimaev-leon-edwards-due-covid-19-dana-white-says|title=Khamzat Chimaev out against Leon Edwards due to COVID-19, Dana White says|work=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2021-02-11|access-date=2021-02-11}} Edwards faced [[Belal Muhammad]] instead.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/02/ufc-news-belal-muhammad-vs-leon-edwards-march-13-replaces-khamzat-chimaev|title=Belal Muhammad steps up, agrees to Leon Edwards fight for UFC main event on March 13|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and Farah Hannoun|date=2021-02-18|access-date=2021-02-18}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between [[Irwin Rivera]] and Ray Rodriguez was scheduled for the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/12/ufc-fight-night-irwin-rivera-vs-ray-rodriguez-booked-march-13|title=Irwin Rivera vs. Ray Rodriguez booked for UFC Fight Night on March 13|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun and Nolan King|date=2020-12-08|access-date=2021-01-08}} However, on January 7, news surfaced that Rivera was arrested for two counts of [[attempted murder]] in [[Palm Beach County, Florida]] after he allegedly stabbed his two sisters. He is currently being held in the Palm Beach County jail without bail.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/platform/amp/2021/1/7/22219633/ufc-fighter-irwin-rivera-jailed-on-charges-of-first-degree-premeditated-murder|title=UFC fighter Irwin Rivera jailed on charges of first-degree premeditated murder|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2021-01-07|access-date=2021-01-07|archive-date=2021-01-08|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20210108033143/https://www.mmafighting.com/platform/amp/2021/1/7/22219633/ufc-fighter-irwin-rivera-jailed-on-charges-of-first-degree-premeditated-murder|url-status=dead}} He was replaced by [[Rani Yahya]].{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/01/ufc-fight-night-ray-rodriguez-vs-rani-yahya-set-for-ufcs-march-13-card|title=UFC Fight Night: Ray Rodriguez vs. Rani Yahya set for UFC's March 13 card|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Simon Head|date=2021-01-23|access-date=2021-01-24}}\n\n[[Tagir Ulanbekov]] and returning veteran [[Matheus Nicolau]] were expected to meet in a flyweight bout at [[UFC 257]], but Ulanbekov withdrew in late December due to undisclosed reasons.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/12/19/22191093/matheus-nicolau-signs-ufc-needs-opponent-ufc-257-tagir-ulanbekov-out|title=Matheus Nicolau re-signs with UFC, needs new opponent for UFC 257 after Tagir Ulanbekov withdraws|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2020-12-19|access-date=2020-12-21}} The pairing was eventually rescheduled for this event.{{cite web|url=https://globoesporte.globo.com/combate/noticia/ultimate-encaminha-remarcacao-de-matheus-nicolau-x-tagir-ulanbekov-para-13-de-marco.ghtml|title=Ultimate sends Matheus Nicolau vs. Tagir Ulanbekov to March 13|publisher=globoesporte.globo.com|author=Raphael Marinho|date=2021-01-12|access-date=2021-01-13}} {{in lang|pt}} However, Ulanbekov pulled out once again and was replaced by former [[Rizin Fighting Federation#Bantamweight Championship|Rizin FF Bantamweight Champion]] [[Manel Kape]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/manel-kape-pakt-short-notice-partij-tegen-matheus-nicolau-op-13-maart/|title=Manel Kape takes short notice match against Matheus Nicolau on March 13|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Staff|date=2021-03-04|access-date=2021-03-04}} {{in lang|nl}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between [[Jairzinho Rozenstruik]] and [[Ciryl Gane]] was originally scheduled for this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2021/1/13/22229560/jairzinho-rozenstruik-vs-cyril-gane-joins-ufc-fight-night-lineup-march-13-mma|title=Jairzinho Rozenstruik vs Cyril Gane joins UFC Fight Night lineup on March 13|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Jesse Holland|date=2021-01-13|access-date=2021-01-30}} However, they were moved to headline [[UFC Fight Night: Rozenstruik vs. Gane]] after the original main event between former [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] challenger [[Dominick Reyes]] and former [[Rizin Fighting Federation#Light Heavyweight Championship|Rizin Light Heavyweight Champion]] [[Jiří Procházka]] was cancelled due to undisclosed reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/01/ufc-news-jairzinho-rozenstruik-ciryl-gane-new-main-event-feb-27|title=Reyes vs. Prochazka out, Rozenstruik vs. Gane in as UFC Fight Night headliner for Feb. 27|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Alexander Mookie|date=2021-01-30|access-date=2021-01-30}}\n\n[[Guram Kutateladze (fighter)|Guram Kutateladze]] was expected to face Don Madge in a lightweight bout at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.essentiallysports.com/mma-ufc-news-guram-kutateladze-announced-to-face-don-madge-on-khamzat-chimaev-vs-leon-edward-card/|title=Guram Kutateladze announced to face Don Madge on Khamzat Chimaev vs Leon Edwards card|publisher=essentiallysports.com|author=Ishaan Bhattacharya|date=2021-01-17|access-date=2021-02-08}} However, Kutateladze pulled out in early February due to a knee injury.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/02/guram-kutateladze-out-march-13-vs-don-madge-knee-injury-ufc|title=Guram Kutateladze says he's out of March 13 UFC fight vs. Don Madge due to knee injury|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2021-02-08|access-date=2021-02-08}} He was replaced by [[Nasrat Haqparast]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/nasrat-haqparast-pakt-short-notice-partij-tegen-don-madge-op-13-maart/|title=Nasrat Haqparast takes short notice match against Don Madge on March 13|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Staff|date=2021-02-11|access-date=2021-02-11}} {{in lang|nl}} In turn, Madge pulled out on March 6 due to [[travel visa|visa]] issues.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/03/ufc-news-don-madge-out-vs-nasrat-haqparast-visa-issues-fight-night|title=Don Madge withdraws from UFC Fight Night 187 vs. Nasrat Haqparast due to visa issues|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2021-03-06|access-date=2021-03-06}} Haqparast eventually faced promotional newcomer [[Rafa García (fighter)|Rafa García]].{{cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2021/03/09/ufc-vegas-21-rafa-garcia-steps-face-nasrat-haqparast/|title=UFC Vegas 21: Rafa Garcia steps in to face Nasrat Haqparast|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Gabriel Gonzalez|date=2021-03-09|access-date=2021-03-09}}\n\n[[The Ultimate Fighter: Team McGregor vs. Team Faber]] lightweight winner [[Ryan Hall (fighter)|Ryan Hall]] was expected to face [[Dan Ige]] in a featherweight bout at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/01/ufc-fight-night-ryan-hall-vs-dan-ige-booked-march-13|title=Dan Ige, Ryan Hall booked for UFC Fight Night on March 13|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Danny Segura|date=2021-01-14|access-date=2021-01-14}} However, Hall pulled out of the fight on February 11 due to undisclosed reasons.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/02/ufc-news-ryan-hall-out-fight-vs-dan-ige-ufc-fight-night-march|title=Ryan Hall out of fight vs. Dan Ige at UFC Fight Night on March 13|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn and Farah Hannoun|date=2021-02-11|access-date=2021-02-11}} Ige faced [[Gavin Tucker]] instead.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/02/ryan-hall-out-gavin-tucker-vs-dan-ige-ufc-fight-night-187-booking-matchup-mma|title=With Ryan Hall out, Gavin Tucker steps in to fight Dan Ige at UFC Fight Night 187|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2021-02-22|access-date=2021-02-22}}\n\n[[Misha Cirkunov]] was expected to face [[Ryan Spann]] in a light heavyweight bout at [[UFC Fight Night: Thompson vs. Neal]]. However, Cirkunov pulled out due to an injury.{{Cite web|url=https://www.ufc.com/news/updates-december-19-ufc-fight-night|title=Updates to December 19 UFC Fight Night|publisher=ufc.com|author=Staff|date=2020-12-05|access-date=2020-12-05}} The fight took place at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/2/18/22289999/misha-cirkunov-vs-ryan-spann-steve-garcia-vs-charles-jourdain-added-to-march-13-ufc-card|title=Misha Cirkunov vs. Ryan Spann re-booked for March 13 UFC card|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2021-02-18|access-date=2021-02-18}}\n\n[[Charles Jourdain]] was expected to face [[Steve Garcia]] in a featherweight bout at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2021/02/18/charles-jourdain-vs-steve-garcia-announced-ufc-march-13/|title=UFC: Charles Jourdain vs. Steve Garcia announced for March 13|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author= Jay Anderson|date=2021-02-18|access-date=2021-02-24}} However, Garcia withdrew from the fight for unknown reasons and was replaced by Marcelo Rojo.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/2/24/22300193/with-steve-garcia-out-charles-jourdain-now-meets-marcelo-rojo-at-ufc-vegas-21|title=With Steve Garcia out, Charles Jourdain now meets Marcelo Rojo at UFC Vegas 21|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck|date=2021-02-24|access-date=2021-02-24}}\n\nA women's strawweight rematch between former [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Strawweight Championship|Invicta FC Strawweight Champion]] [[Angela Hill]] and [[Ashley Yoder]] was expected to take place at [[UFC Fight Night: Rozenstruik vs. Gane]].{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/02/angela-hill-vs-ashley-yoder-ufc-fight-night-186-mma-matchup-booking|title=Angela Hill vs. Ashley Yoder late addition to UFC Fight Night 186|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2021-02-20|access-date=2021-02-27}} The two met before at [[The Ultimate Fighter: Redemption#The Ultimate Fighter 25 Finale|The Ultimate Fighter: Redemption Finale]] in July 2017, with Hill winning via unanimous decision.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2017/07/tuf-25-finale-results-angela-hill-unanimous-win-over-ashley-yoder|title=TUF 25 Finale results: Angela Hill's standup game shines in unanimous win over Ashley Yoder|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Simon Samano|date=2017-07-07|access-date=2021-02-27}} Their second meeting was postponed on the day of that event, as it was announced that one of Yoder's cornermen tested positive for COVID-19. The bout took place at this card.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/2/27/22304718/angela-hill-vs-ashley-yoder-2-postponed-due-to-covid-19-protocols-moved-to-ufc-vegas-21|title=Angela Hill vs. Ashley Yoder 2 postponed due to COVID-19 protocols, moved to UFC Vegas 21|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2021-02-27|access-date=2021-02-27}}\n\nA featherweight bout between [[Ricardo Ramos (fighter)|Ricardo Ramos]] and [[Zubaira Tukhugov]] was expected to take place during the preliminary portion of the event.{{cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2021/01/18/ufc-zubaira-tukhugov-vs-ricardo-ramos-booked-march-13/|title=UFC: Zubaira Tukhugov vs. Ricardo Ramos booked for March 13|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Jay Anderson|date=2021-01-18|access-date=2021-03-06}} A week before the event, Tukhugov pulled out due to undisclosed reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com.br/mma/artigo/_/id/8276444/carcacinha-fica-sem-adversario-e-tem-participacao-no-ufc-vegas-21-ameacada|title='Carcacinha' is without an opponent and his participation in UFC Vegas 21 is threatened|publisher=espn.com.br|author=Staff|date=2021-03-04|access-date=2021-03-06}} {{in lang|pt}} Promotion officials elected to remove Ramos from the card entirely and he will be rescheduled for a future event.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/3/9/22321413/no-opponent-available-ricardo-ramos-pulled-ufc-vegas-21|title=With no opponent available, Ricardo Ramos pulled from UFC Vegas 21|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2021-03-09|access-date=2021-03-09}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between [[Ben Rothwell]] and [[Philipe Lins]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Report-Ben-Rothwell-Takes-on-Philipe-Lins-at-UFC-Fight-Night-187-on-March-13-177452|title=Ben Rothwell takes on Philipe Lins at UFC Fight Night 187 on March 13|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Jay Pettry|date=2021-01-29|access-date=2021-03-09}} However, during the week leading up to the event the bout was removed from the card due to undisclosed reasons. The pairing is expected to remain intact and rescheduled for [[UFC on ESPN: Rodriguez vs. Waterson]].{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/3/9/22321889/ben-rothwell-vs-philipe-lins-fight-at-ufc-vegas-21-postponed|title=Ben Rothwell vs. Philipe Lins fight at UFC Vegas 21 postponed|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz and Damon Martin|date=2021-03-09|access-date=2021-03-09}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card (ESPN+)}}\n{{MMAevent [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], [[United States]].{{Cite month|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Newswire|date=2021-01-20|access-date=2021-01-20}}\n\n== Background ==\nA [[UFC Welterweight Championship]] bout between the current champion [[Kamaru Usman]] (also [[The Ultimate Fighter: American Top Team vs. Blackzilians]] welterweight winner) and [[Gilbert Burns]] served as the event headliner.{{Cite web|url=https://www.essentiallysports.com/ufc-mma-news-reports-kamaru-usman-will-defend-his-welterweight-title-on-february-13-gilbert-burns/|title=Kamaru Usman will defend his welterweight title on February 13|publisher=essentiallysports.com|author=Rishabh Singh|date=2020-11-17|access-date=2020-11-17}} The duo was previously scheduled to meet in July 2020 at [[UFC 251]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/06/ufc-251-kamaru-usman-vs-gilbert-burns-booked-to-headline|title=Kamaru Usman vs. Gilbert Burns headlines UFC 251 on 'Fight Island' in Abu Dhabi|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2020-06-09|access-date=2020-11-17}} However, it was announced on July 3 that Burns was pulled from the contest after he and his coach, Greg Jones, [[COVID-19 testing|tested positive for COVID-19]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/07/ufc-251-gilbert-burns-out-kamaru-usman-title-fight-coronavirus|title=Gilbert Burns tests positive for COVID-19, out of UFC 251 headliner vs. Kamaru Usman|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2020-07-03|access-date=2020-11-17}} They were also briefly targeted to headline [[UFC 256]], but Usman pulled out citing more time needed to recover from undisclosed injuries.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/9/1/21417296/kamaru-usman-vs-gilbert-burns-targeted-for-ufc-256-in-december|title=Kamaru Usman vs. Gilbert Burns targeted for UFC 256 in December|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2020-09-01|access-date=2020-11-17}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/30048745/sources-kamaru-usman-gilbert-burns-fight-happen-dec-12|title=Sources: Usman-Burns fight on Dec. 12 is off|work=espn.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2020-10-05|access-date=2020-11-17}}\n\nA middleweight bout between former interim [[UFC Middleweight Championship]] challenger [[Kelvin Gastelum]] (also [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Jones vs. Team Sonnen]] middleweight winner) and [[Ian Heinisch]] was originally expected to take place at a planned event on January 30.{{Cite web|url=https://www.bloodyelbow.com/2020/11/13/21563229/ufc-news-report-kelvin-gastelum-vs-ian-heinisch-added-to-ufc-fight-night-in-january|title=Report: Kelvin Gastelum vs. Ian Heinisch added to UFC However, the UFC opted against holding a Fight Night on that date and rescheduled the contest to this card.{{Cite web|url=https://mmainsight.com/ufc/ufc-scraps-plans-for-january-30th-fight-night-event|title=UFC scraps plans for January 30th Fight Night event|publisher=mmainsight.com|author=Ross (fighter)|Anthony Hernandez]] was expected to take place at [[UFC Fight Night: Holloway vs. Kattar]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/bjj-specialist-rodolfo-vieira-treft-anthony-hernandez-tijdens-ufc-evenement-op-16-januari/|title=BJJ specialist Rodolfo Vieira meets Anthony Hernandez at UFC event on January event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Positive-COVID19-Test-for-Anthony-Hernandez-Pushes-Rodolfo-Vieira-Match-to-Feb-13-177009|title=Positive COVID-19 test for Anthony Hernandez also expected to take place at the planned January 30 date.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/11/ufc-fight-night-pedro-munhoz-vs-jimmie-rivera-2-booked-jan-30|title=Pedro Munhoz vs. Night: Belfort vs. Henderson 3]] in November 2015, when Rivera won via split decision.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2015/11/ufc-fight-night-77-results-jimmie-rivera-takes-split-decision-from-pedro-munhoz|title=UFC Fight Night 77 results: Jimmie Rivera takes split decision from Pedro Munhoz|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Ben Fowlkes|date=2015-11-07|access-date=2020-12-26}} The pairing was then rescheduled for [[UFC on ESPN: Chiesa vs. Magny]], before being moved once again, as they were booked to this event due to undisclosed reasons.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/1/7/22218566/pedro-munhoz-vs-jimmie-rivera-rematch-rescheduled-for-ufc-258|title=Pedro Munhoz vs. Jimmie Rivera rematch rescheduled for UFC 258|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck and Guilherme Cruz|date=2021-01-07|access-date=2021-01-07}} During the week leading up to the event, the bout was delayed again due to a positive COVID-19 test for someone within the two camps. The pairing remained intact and was rescheduled for two weeks later at [[UFC Fight Night: Rozenstruik vs. Gane]].{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/2/9/22274782/pedro-munhoz-jimmie-rivera-off-ufc-258-rebooked-ufc-later-date|title=Pedro Munhoz vs. Jimmie Rivera off UFC 258, fight to be rescheduled for later expected to take place in December 2020 at [[UFC Fight Night: Thompson vs. Neal]].{{Cite Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite Acedera|date=2021-01-13|access-date=2021-01-24}}\n\n==Background==\nA heavyweight bout between [[Curtis Blaydes]] and former [[UFC Heavyweight Championship]] challenger [[Derrick Lewis]] headlined this event.{{cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/30652416/curtis-blaydes-derrick-lewis-fight-now-set-headline-ufc-fight-night-feb-20-sources-say|title=Curtis Blaydes-Derrick Lewis fight now set to headline UFC Fight Night on Feb. 20, sources say|work=espn.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2021-01-04|access-date=2021-01-04}} They were initially scheduled to serve as the headliner for [[UFC on ESPN: Smith vs. Clark]] in November, but the contest was scrapped due to Blaydes [[COVID-19 testing|testing positive for COVID-19]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/11/curtis-blaydes-tests-positive-covid-19-ufc-on-espn-18-main-event-canceled|title=Curtis Blaydes tests positive for COVID-19, UFC on ESPN 18 main event scrapped|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2020-11-27|access-date=2021-01-04}}\n\nA women's bantamweight bout between [[Ketlen Vieira]] and former [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Bantamweight Championship|Invicta FC Bantamweight Champion]] and [[UFC Women's Featherweight Championship]] challenger [[Yana Kunitskaya]] took place at this event.{{Cite Vieira weighed in at 138 pounds, two pounds over the women's bantamweight non-title fight limit. Gordon weighed in at 150 pounds, four pounds over the featherweight non-title fight limit. Rodriguez weighed in at 140.5 pounds, four and a half pounds over the bantamweight non-title fight limit. Laramie out of May 1 UFC bout against Damon Jackson|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Newswire|date=2021-03-31|access-date=2021-04-01}}{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/luke-sanders-pakt-short-notice-gevecht-in-de-ufc-tegen-damon-jackson-op-1-mei/|title=Luke Sanders takes short notice fight in the UFC against Damon Jackson on May 1|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Staff|date=2021-04-08|access-date=2021-04-09}} {{in lang|nl}} On April 22, it was Jackson that pulled out due to an injury. [[Journey Newson]], who was expected to face [[Felipe Colares]] in a bantamweight bout, also pulled out due to injury. The promotion decided to match Sanders and Corales as a result of those removals.{{Cite web|url=https://tudosobremma.com/exclusivo-felipe-cabocao-recebe-novo-adversario/|title=Exclusive! Carolina weighed in 128.5 pounds, 2.5 pounds over the women’s flyweight non-title limit. [[Stipe Miocic]] and [[Francis Ngannou]] headlined this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/01/ufc-260-stipe-miocic-vs-francis-ngannou-rematch-booked-heavyweight-title|title=Stipe web|url=https://sports.yahoo.com/ufc-220-stipe-miocic-dominates-francis-ngannou-retains-heavyweight-belt-062103147.html|title=UFC 220: Stipe Miocic dominates Francis Ngannou, retains heavyweight belt|publisher=sports.yahoo.com|author=Kevin Iole|date=2018-01-21|access-date=2021-01-17}}\n\nA [[UFC Featherweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Alexander Volkanovski]] and former title challenger [[Brian Ortega]] would have served as the co-main event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/3/20/22342440/alexander-volkanovski-out-of-ufc-260-due-covid-19-protocols-fight-against-brian-ortega-rescheduled|title=Alexander Volkanovski out of UFC 260 due to COVID-19 protocols, fight against Brian Ortega to be rescheduled|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2021-03-20|access-date=2021-03-20}} The pairing was later rescheduled for [[UFC 266]].{{Cite web|date=2021-07-14|title=Volkanovski, Shevchenko to defend at UFC 266|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/31814276/alexander-volkanovski-valentina-shevchenko-set-defend-titles-ufc-266|access-date=2021-07-29|website=ESPN.com|language=en}}\n\n[[Johnny Walker (fighter)|Johnny Walker]] was expected to face [[Jimmy Crute]] in a light heavyweight bout at the event.{{Cite Smith (fighter)|Anthony Smith]] the following month at [[UFC 261]].{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/02/ufc-261-anthony-smith-replaces-johnny-walker-meets-jimmy-crute|title=Anthony [[List of WSOF champions#Heavyweight Championship|WSOF Heavyweight Champion]] [[Blagoy Ivanov]] and [[Marcin Tybura]] was scheduled to take place at the event.{{cite Fight Night: Rozenstruik vs. Sakai]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Font vs. Garbrandt''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 188''''', '''''UFC on ESPN+ 46''''' and '''''UFC Vegas 27''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on May 22, 2021, at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://www.ufc.com/news/rob-font-set-battle-cody-garbrandt-bantamweight-ufc-fight-night-read|title=Rob Font set to web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/4/1/22362917/ufc-requests-four-events-for-may-at-ufc-apex-in-las-vegas|title=UFC requests four events for May at UFC APEX in Las Vegas|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2021-04-01|access-date=2021-04-01}}\n\n==Background==\nA bantamweight bout between former [[UFC Bantamweight Championship|UFC Bantamweight Champion]] [[Cody Garbrandt]] and [[Rob Font]] served as the main event.{{Cite web|url=https://empiresportsmedia.com/mma/breaking-ufc-booking-main-event-between-cody-garbrandt-and-rob-font/|title=BREAKING: UFC booking main event between Cody Garbrandt and Rob Font|publisher=empiresportsmedia.com|author=Daniel Cunningham|date=2021-03-18|access-date=2021-03-19}}\n\n[[Ricardo Ramos (fighter)|Ricardo Ramos]] and [[Bill Algeo]] were originally scheduled to meet in a bantamweight bout five weeks earlier at [[UFC on ESPN: Whittaker vs. Gastelum]], but Ramos was pulled from the fight during the week leading up to the event after he [[COVID-19 testing|tested positive for COVID-19]].{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/4/14/22384623/ricardo-ramos-withdraws-ufc-vegas-24-positive-covid-19-test-bill-algeo|title=Ricardo Ramos withdraws from UFC Vegas 24 after positive COVID-19 test|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz and Mike Heck |date=2021-04-14|access-date=2021-04-14}} The pairing was left intact and took place at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/04/ufc-news-bill-algeo-vs-ricardo-ramos-rebooked-fight-night-may-22|title=After COVID-19 cancellation, Bill Algeo vs. Ricardo Ramos added to UFC Fight Night 188 on May 22|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and Farah Hannoun|date=2021-04-21|access-date=2021-04-22}}\n\nDenys Bondar was expected to face Victor Rodriguez in a flyweight bout at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.overtimeheroics.net/2021/03/02/rodriguez-and-denys-bondar/|title=Victor Rodriguez and Denys Bondar meet at UFC Fight Night 192|publisher=overtimeheroics.net|author=Jake Broderick|date=2021-03-02|access-date=2021-04-30}} However, Bondar was forced to pull out in late April due to an injury and was replaced by [[Bruno Gustavo da Silva]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.explica.co/bondar-injured-bruno-silva-faces-victor-rodriguez-at-ufc-fight-night-on-may-22.html|title=Bondar injured, Bruno Silva faces Victor Rodriguez at UFC Fight Night on May newcomer [[Danyelle Wolf]] was expected to face former [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Featherweight Championship|Invicta FC Featherweight Champion]] and [[UFC Women's Featherweight Championship]] challenger [[Felicia Spencer]] in a women's featherweight bout at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/2/22/22296101/felicia-spencer-vs-danyelle-wolf-targeted-for-may-22-ufc-event|title=Felicia Spencer vs. Danyelle Wolf targeted for May 22 UFC event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee and Damon Martin|date=2021-02-22|access-date=2021-05-06}} However, Wolf pulled out of the fight in early May due to an undisclosed injury and was replaced by [[Norma Dumont]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/05/danyelle-wolf-out-felicia-spencer-vs-norma-dumont-ufc-fight-night-188-women-mma-matchup|title=With Danyelle Wolf out, Felicia Spencer meets Norma Dumont at UFC Fight Night 188|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2021-05-06|access-date=2021-05-06}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between [[Rodrigo Nascimento]] and [[Alan Baudot]] was originally expected to take place at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://lasueur.com/alan-baudot-ufc-28-2-2021|title=French Alan Baudot returns to the UFC on May 22!|publisher=lasueur.com|author=Staff|date=2021-02-28|access-date=2021-05-09}} {{in lang|fr}} However, they were rescheduled for [[UFC on ESPN: Makhachev vs. Moisés]] after Baudot got injured.{{Cite web|url=https://fighterpath.com/2021/05/07/breaking-heavyweight-bout-between-alan-baudot-and-rodrigo-nascimiento-pushed-to-july-17/|title=BREAKING: Heavyweight bout between Alan Baudot and Rodrigo originally expected to take place one week prior at [[UFC 262]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/3/20/22341466/jack-hermansson-vs-edmen-shahbazyan-booked-for-ufc-262|title=Jack Hermansson vs. Edmen Shahbazyan booked for UFC 262|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2021-03-20|access-date=2021-05-10}} However, the bout was postponed due to a [[COVID-19]] case in Hermansson's camp and took place at this event instead.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/05/ufc-262-news-jack-hermansson-vs-edmen-shabazyan-shifts-fight-night-188|title=UFC 262 loses Jack Hermansson vs. Edmen Shahbazyan; bout pushed back one week|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2021-05-10|access-date=2021-05-10}}\n\n[[Ben Rothwell]] and [[Philipe Lins]] were scheduled to meet in a heavyweight bout in March at [[UFC Fight Night: Edwards vs. Muhammad]], but it was cancelled due to an injury to Rothwell.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/3/9/22321889/ben-rothwell-vs-philipe-lins-fight-at-ufc-vegas-21-postponed|title=Ben Rothwell vs. Philipe Lins fight at UFC Vegas 21 fell through once again as Lins got ill a day before their fight.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/05/ufc-on-espn-24-philipe-lins-withdraws-fight-illness-ben-rothwell|title=Philipe Lins out of UFC on ESPN 24 fight with Ben Rothwell because of illness|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2021-05-07|access-date=2021-05-07}} They were then expected to compete at replaced by promotional newcomer Askar Mozharov.{{cite web|url=https://fighttime.ru/news/item/24871-askar-mozharov-vstretitsya-s-benom-rotvellom.html|title=Askar Mozharov will meet with Ben Rothwell|publisher=fighttime.ru|author=Staff|date=2021-05-12|access-date=2021-05-12}} {{in lang|ru}} Just hours later, the Ukrainian subsequently announced that he would not be able to get a [[Travel [[UFC on ESPN: Whittaker vs. Gastelum]]\n|followingevent= [[UFC on ESPN: Reyes vs. Procházka]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC 261: Usman vs. Masvidal 2''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on April 24, 2021 at [[VyStar Veterans Memorial Arena]] in [[Jacksonville, Florida|Jacksonville]], Florida, United States.{{Cite web|url=https://globoesporte.globo.com/combate/noticia/sem-acordo-para-realizar-ufc-261-em-singapura-ultimate-opta-por-las-vegas.ghtml|title=Without agreement to perform UFC 261 in Singapore, UFC opts for Las Vegas|publisher=globoesporte.globo.com|author=Raphael Marinho|date=2021-02-18|access-date=2021-02-18}} {{in lang|pt}}{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/03/ufc-261-jacksonville-florida-kamaru-usman-vs-jorge-masvidal-2-full-crowd-shevchenko-andrade-weili-namajunas|title=UFC 261 heads to Florida with Usman-Masvidal 2 leading three title fights in front of full crowd|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2021-03-15|access-date=2021-03-15}}\n\n==Background==\nThe promotion had initially planned to hold the event in [[Singapore]]. However, negotiations with the Singaporean government fizzled amongst [[COVID-19 pandemic in Singapore|COVID-19 protocols]] and the promotion initially turned to their home base of Las Vegas. On March 15, UFC president [[Dana White]] announced the event would be moved to [[Jacksonville, Florida|Jacksonville]], Florida, with a full capacity of \"15,000 UFC fans\", marking the first time since [[UFC 248]] in March 2020 that a full capacity crowd would be available (the UFC hosted a trio of events in [[Abu Dhabi]] in January with a limited number of fans). Jacksonville also hosted the UFC's first fights back after the start of the [[COVID-19 pandemic]] in May 2020, before the organization started hosting events in either Las Vegas or Abu Dhabi.\n\nA [[UFC Welterweight Championship]] rematch between the current champion [[Kamaru Usman]] (also [[The Ultimate Fighter: American Top Team vs. Blackzilians]] welterweight winner) and [[Jorge Masvidal]] headlined the event. They previously met at [[UFC 251]], where Usman defended his title via unanimous decision.{{cite web|url=https://www.dazn.com/en-GLOBAL/news/mma/ufc-251-usman-vs-masvidal-fight-island-results-live-updates-highlights-full-card/16p3thlge05d018iil3yxb3due|title=UFC 251 results: Kamaru Usman dominates Jorge Masvidal to retain UFC Welterweight title|publisher=dazn.com|author=Andreas Hale|date=2020-07-14|access-date=2021-03-16}}\n\nA [[UFC Women's Strawweight Championship]] bout between the current champion [[Zhang Weili]] and former champion [[Rose Namajunas]] took place as the co-main event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/30970542/zhang-weili-vs-rose-namajunas-strawweight-title-being-targeted-ufc-261|title=Sources: Zhang Weili vs. Rose Namajunas for strawweight title being targeted for UFC 261|work=espn.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2021-02-26|access-date=2021-03-12}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.scmp.com/sport/martial-arts/mixed-martial-arts/article/3124621/ufc-261-zhang-weili-confirmed-fight-rose|title=UFC 261: Zhang Weili confirmed to fight Rose Namajunas on April 24 in strawweight title clash|publisher=scmp.com|author=Mathew Scott|date=2021-03-09|access-date=2021-03-12}}\n\nA [[UFC Women's Flyweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Valentina Shevchenko]] and former strawweight champion [[Jéssica Andrade]] completed the triad of title fights.{{Cite
