Whittaker vs. Gastelum]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC on ABC: Vettori vs. Holland''''' (also known as '''''UFC on ABC 2''''' and '''''UFC Vegas 23''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on April 10, 2021 at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/3/11/22325713/darren-till-vs-marvin-vettori-main-card-moves-to-abc-on-april-10-with-earlier-start-time|title=Darren Till vs. Marvin Vettori main card moves to ABC on April 10 with earlier start time|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Newswire|date=2021-03-11|access-date=2021-03-12}}\n\n==Background==\nThis was the first event to feature [[Venum]] as the new UFC's official uniform partner. The promotion had previously been partnered with [[Reebok]] since 2014, before signing a deal with Venum in July 2020 that would be made effective April 2021.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/31176357/ufc-new-venum-uniform-deal-gives-fighters-slight-pay-bump|title=UFC's new Venum uniform deal gives fighters slight pay bump|publisher=espn.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2021-04-01|access-date=2021-04-01}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/4/1/22361946/ufc-reveals-new-pay-tiers-for-fighters-under-venum-partnership-after-reebok-era-comes-to-an-end|title=UFC reveals new pay tiers for fighters under Venum partnership after Reebok era comes to an end|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2021-04-01|access-date=2021-04-01}}\n\nA middleweight bout between former [[UFC Welterweight Championship]] challenger [[Darren Till]] and [[Marvin Vettori]] was expected to serve as the event headliner.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/01/ufc-fight-night-darren-till-vs-marvin-vettori-set-april-10-booking|title=Darren Till vs. Marvin Vettori targeted to headline UFC Fight Night on April 10|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and Mike Bohn|date=2021-01-26|access-date=2021-01-26}} However, Till withdrew from the fight on March 30 due to a broken collarbone.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/31167414/darren-ufc-main-event-vs-marvin-vettori-april-10-due-broken-collarbone|title=Injured Till out of April 10 main event vs. Vettori|publisher=espn.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2021-03-30|access-date=2021-03-30}} It was announced on April 1 that [[Kevin Holland]] would step in on 9 days notice to face Vettori. By making his second main event appearance in three weeks, Holland tied the record for shortest turnaround between two main events.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/31179539/kevin-holland-steps-fight-marvin-vettori-ufc-fight-night-april-10|title=Sources: Holland steps in for Till to fight Vettori|publisher=espn.com|author=Marc Raimondi and Ariel Helwani|date=2021-04-01|access-date=2021-04-01}}\n\nA middleweight bout between [[Zak Cummings]] and [[Sam Alvey]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/01/zak-cummings-vs-sam-alvey-added-to-ufc-fight-night-on-april-10|title=Zak Cummings vs. Sam Alvey added to UFC Fight Night on April 10|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2021-01-22|access-date=2021-03-11}} However, Cummings withdrew from the bout on March 11 due to undisclosed reasons and was replaced by [[Julian Marquez]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/03/zak-cummings-out-julian-marquez-vs-sam-alvey-ufc-on-abc-2|title=With Zak Cummings out, Julian Marquez steps in to face Sam Alvey at UFC on ABC 2 on April 10|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2021-03-11|access-date=2021-03-11}}\n\nA light heavyweight bout between [[Shamil Gamzatov]] and [[Da Un Jung]] was scheduled for the preliminary portion of the card.{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/da-un-jung-treft-ongeslagen-shamil-gamzatov-tijdens-ufc-evenement-op-10-april/|title=Da Un Jung faces undefeated Shamil Gamzatov at UFC event on April 10|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Staff|date=2021-02-03|access-date=2021-03-24}} {{in lang|nl}} However, Gamzatov was removed from the bout on March 24 due to alleged [[Travel visa|visa]] issues and replaced by [[William Knight (fighter)|William Knight]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/03/ufc-260-news-william-knight-covid-19-alonzo-menifield-vs-fabio-cherant|title=William Knight out of UFC 260 due to COVID-19 protocols; Alonzo Menifield meets Fabio Cherant|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2021-03-24|access-date=2021-03-24}}\n\n[[Bea Malecki]] was expected to face [[Norma Dumont]] in a women's bantamweight bout at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.kimura.se/ufc-fight-night-188-bea-malecki-norma-dumont/|title=UFC Fight Night 188: Bea Malecki confirms match against Norma Dumont|publisher=kimura.se|author=Alexander Clausson|date=2021-02-24|access-date=2021-04-01}} {{in lang|sv}} However, Malecki pulled out a week before the contest due to undisclosed reasons and was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Erin Blanchfield]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/4/1/22362114/top-prospect-erin-blanchfield-steps-in-for-bea-malecki-to-fight-norma-dumont-at-april-10-ufc-show|title=Top prospect Erin Blanchfield steps in for Bea Malecki to fight Norma Dumont at April 10 UFC show|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee and Damon Martin|date=2021-04-01|access-date=2021-04-01}}\n\nA middleweight bout between [[Kyle Daukaus]] and Aliaskhab Khizriev was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/3/9/22321516/kyle-daukaus-vs-aliaskhab-khizriev-set-for-april-10-ufc-event|title=Kyle Daukaus vs. Aliaskhab Khizriev set for April 10 UFC event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2021-03-09|access-date=2021-04-08}} However, the bout was pulled from the card on April 7 due to [[COVID-19]] protocols.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/4/7/22371812/kyle-daukaus-vs-aliaskhab-khizriev-pulled-from-ufc-vegas-23|title=Kyle Daukaus vs. Aliaskhab Khizriev pulled from UFC Vegas 23|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2021-04-07|access-date=2021-04-07}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, [[Ignacio Bahamondes]] and [[Norma Dumont]] missed weight for the respective bouts. Bahamondes weighed in at 156.75 pounds, three quarters of a pound over the lightweight non-title fight limit. His bout proceeded at a catchweight and he was fined 20% of his individual purse, which went to his opponent [[John Makdessi]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/04/ufc-abc-2-marvin-vettori-kevin-holland-weigh-in-results-live-video-stream|title=UFC on ABC 2 weigh-in results: One fight scratched at scales|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=John Morgan|date=2021-04-09|access-date=2021-04-10}} Dumont weighed in at 139.5 pounds, three and a half pounds over the women's bantamweight non-title fight limit. Zombie vs. Ige]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC 263: Adesanya vs. Vettori 2''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on June 12, 2021, at the [[Gila River Arena]] in [[Glendale, Arizona]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/30962174/ufc-flyweight-champion-deiveson-figueiredo-again-fight-brandon-moreno-june-12-sources-say|title=UFC flyweight champion Deiveson Figueiredo to again fight Brandon Moreno on June 12|work=espn.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2021-02-25|access-date=2021-02-25}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.azcentral.com/story/sports/2021/05/02/ufc-263-coming-glendale-june-12/4899057001/|title=UFC 263 coming to Gila River Arena in Glendale on June 12|publisher=azcentral.com|author=Richard Obert|date=2021-05-01|access-date=2021-05-01}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was the second that the promotion has contested in Glendale, following [[UFC on Fox: Poirier vs. Gaethje]] in April 2018. [[Zuffa]] had previously hosted the final [[World Extreme Cagefighting]] event, [[WEC 53]], at the arena in December 2010.\n\nA [[UFC Middleweight Championship]] bout between then champion [[Israel Adesanya]] and [[Marvin Vettori]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/31324008/ufc-finalizing-middleweight-title-fight-israel-adesanya-marvin-vettori-dana-white-says|title=UFC finalizing middleweight title fight between Israel Adesanya and Marvin Vettori, Dana White says|work=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2021-04-24|access-date=2021-04-24}} The pair previously met at [[UFC on Fox: Poirier vs. Gaethje]], where Adesanya won via split decision.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2018/4/14/17238686/ufc-on-fox-29-results-israel-adesanya-vs-marvin-vettori-mma-glendale|title=UFC on Fox 29 results: Israel Adesanya outlasts Marvin Vettori for first career decision win|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Andrew Pearson|date=2018-04-14|access-date=2021-04-24}}\n\nA [[UFC Flyweight Championship]] rematch between champion [[Deiveson Figueiredo]] and former title challenger [[Brandon Moreno]] took place as the co-main event. The pairing previously met in December 2020 at [[UFC 256]], where Figueiredo retained the title after the bout was scored a majority draw.{{Cite web|url=https://www.cbssports.com/mma/news/ufc-256-results-highlights-deiveson-figueiredo-brandon-moreno-fight-to-a-draw-in-an-instant-classic/live/|title=UFC 256 results, highlights: Deiveson Figueiredo, Brandon Moreno fight to a draw in an instant classic|publisher=cbssports.com|author=Brent Brookhouse|date=2020-12-13|access-date=2021-02-25}}\n\nA light heavyweight bout between [[Paul Craig]] and [[Jamahal Hill]] took place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/meerdere-ufc-partijen-bekend-gemaakt-inclusief-craig-vs-hill-terugkeer-jacare/|title=Multiple UFC fights announced, including Craig vs. Hill|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Staff|date=2021-03-24|access-date=2021-03-24}} {{in lang|nl}} The pairing was previously scheduled to take place at [[UFC on ESPN: Brunson vs. Holland]], but Hill withdrew from that event on March 10 after [[COVID-19 testing|testing positive for COVID-19]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/03/ufc-on-espn-21-paul-craig-confirms-jamahal-hill-bout-canceled|title=UFC on ESPN 21 co-main event scrapped after Jamahal Hill forced to withdraw from Paul Craig bout|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farrah Hannoun|date=2021-03-10|access-date=2021-03-10}}\n\nA welterweight fight between [[Leon Edwards]] and former [[UFC Lightweight Championship]] challenger [[Nate Diaz]] (also [[The Ultimate Fighter 5]] lightweight winner) was expected to take place at [[UFC 262]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/03/ufc-262-news-nate-diaz-vs-leon-edwards-five-rounds-co-main-event-houston|title=UFC 262 books Nate Diaz vs. Leon Edwards for five-round co-main event in Houston|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and Mike Bohn|date=2021-03-30|access-date=2021-03-30}} However, Diaz pulled out due to a minor injury in early May and the bout took place at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/5/3/22418618/leon-edwards-vs-nate-diaz-rescheduled-from-ufc-262-in-may-to-ufc-263-in-june|title=Leon Edwards vs. Nate Diaz rescheduled from UFC 262 in May to UFC 263 in June|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2021-05-03|access-date=2021-05-03}} Despite the different date, the pairing retained its original status, marking the first time in UFC history that a non-title bout other than the main event was scheduled for five rounds.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/31386292/nate-diaz-battling-minor-injury-fight-vs-leon-edwards-moved-ufc-263-june|title=Nate Diaz battling minor injury; fight vs. Leon Edwards moved to UFC 263 in June|work=espn.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2021-05-04|access-date=2021-05-06}}\n\nA women's bantamweight bout between [[Karol Rosa]] and [[Sijara Eubanks]] was scheduled to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://globoesporte.globo.com/combate/noticia/ultimate-acerta-duelo-entre-karol-rosa-e-sijara-eubanks-para-o-ufc-263-dia-12-de-junho.ghtml|title=UFC sets duel between Karol Rosa and Sijara Eubanks for UFC 263, June 12|publisher=globoesporte.globo.com|author=Raphael Marinho|date=2021-03-18|access-date=2021-05-28}} {{in lang|pt}} However, Rosa pulled out of the fight in late-May citing an eye injury. In turn, Eubanks was removed from the card and will face [[Priscila Cachoeira]] six weeks later at [[UFC Fight Night: Sandhagen vs. Dillashaw]] instead.{{Cite web|url=https://globoesporte.globo.com/combate/noticia/ufc-karol-rosa-lesiona-o-olho-e-pedrita-a-substitui-contra-sijara-eubanks.ghtml|title=UFC: Karol Rosa injures her eye and Pedrita replaces her against Sijara Eubanks|publisher=globoesporte.globo.com|author=Raphael Marinho and Zeca Azevedo|date=2021-05-28|access-date=2021-05-28}} {{in lang|pt}}\n\nA lightweight bout between [[Frank Camacho]] and [[Matt Frevola]] was originally scheduled to take place at [[UFC on ESPN: Blaydes vs. Volkov]] in June 2020, but Frevola was pulled from the fight during the week leading up to the event after teammate and [[cornerman]] [[Billy Quarantillo]] tested positive for COVID-19. Despite two negative tests, Frevola was removed from the bout in an abundance of caution.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/06/ufc-espn-11-matt-frevola-out-billy-quarantillo-positive-covid-test|title=Matt Frevola out of UFC on ESPN 11 after cornerman Billy Quarantillo tests positive for COVID-19|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2020-06-18|access-date=2020-06-18}} They were then rescheduled for this event.{{cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2021/04/18/frank-camacho-vs-matt-frevola-set-ufc-263/|title=Frank Camacho vs. Matt Frevola set for UFC 263|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Alex Behunin|date=2021-04-18|access-date=2021-06-08}} In turn, Camacho pulled out of the fight four days before the event after being injured in a car accident. Frevola faced promotional newcomer [[Terrance McKinney]] instead.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/06/ufc-263-frank-camacho-car-accident-matt-frevola-vs-terrance-mckinney|title=Frank Camacho in car accident, out of UFC 263; Terrance McKinney agrees to fight Matt Frevola|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Matthew Wells, John Morgan and Nolan King|date=2021-06-08|access-date=2021-06-08}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, [[Steven Peterson]] weighed in at 148.5 pounds, two and a half pounds over the featherweight non-title fight limit. His bout proceeded at catchweight and he was fined 20% of his purse, which went to his opponent [[Chase Hooper]].{{Cite web|url= https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/6/11/22529770/ufc-263-weigh-in-results-deiveson-figueiredo-makes-weight-at-last-minute-flyweight-championship-bout|title= UFC 263 weigh-in results: Deiveson Figueiredo makes weight at last minute for flyweight championship bout|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on May 8, 2021, at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada|Enterprise]], Nevada, part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://www.ufc.com/news/sandhagen-vs-dillashaw-set-may-8|title=Sandhagen vs. Dillashaw set for May 8|publisher=ufc.com|author=Staff|date=2021-03-19|access-date=2021-03-19}}{{Cite Vegas|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2021-04-01|access-date=2021-04-01}}\n\n==Background==\nA bantamweight bout between former two-time [[UFC Bantamweight Championship|UFC Bantamweight Champion]] [[T.J. Dillashaw]] and [[Cory Sandhagen]] was expected to serve as the main event.{{cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/31090537/former-ufc-champ-tj-dillashaw-make-return-cory-sandhagen-sources-say|title=Former UFC champ TJ Dillashaw to make return in May against Cory Sandhagen, sources say|publisher=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2021-03-19|access-date=2021-03-19}} It would be Dillashaw's first fight since his two-year suspension due to testing positive for [[recombinant human erythropoietin]] (EPO), which resulted in his title vacancy.{{cite web|url=http://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/26313639|title=Dillashaw gives up belt, suspended by NYSAC|publisher=espn.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2019-03-20|access-date=2021-03-19}}{{cite web|url=http://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/26479192/usada-suspends-dillashaw-2-years-epo-use|title=USADA suspends Dillashaw 2 years for EPO use|publisher=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2019-04-09|access-date=2021-03-19}} However, Dillashaw announced on April 27 that he had to pull out of the fight due to a cut he received from a headbutt in training.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/04/ufc-news-tj-dillashaw-facial-cut-out-ufc-main-event-cory-sandhagen|title=Facial cut forces T.J. Dillashaw from UFC comeback fight vs. Cory Sandhagen|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2021-04-27|access-date=2021-04-28}} Sandhagen was removed from the card as well, with the pairing remaining intact and rescheduled at a future event.{{cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/TJ-Dillashaw-Out-of-UFC-on-ESPN-24-Waterson-vs-Rodriguez-Targeted-as-New-Main-Event-179045|title=T.J. Dillashaw out of UFC on ESPN 24; Waterson vs. Rodriguez targeted as new main event|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Tristen Critchfield|date=2021-04-28|access-date=2021-04-28}} A women's flyweight bout between former [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Atomweight Championship|Invicta FC Atomweight Champion]] [[Michelle Waterson]] and [[Marina Rodriguez]] served as the new main event instead.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/4/27/22406742/t-j-dillashaw-injured-ufc-targets-michelle-waterson-vs-marina-rodriguez-to-headline-ufc-vegas-26|title=T.J. Dillashaw injured, UFC targets Michelle Waterson vs. Marina Rodriguez to headline UFC Vegas 26|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2021-04-27|access-date=2021-04-28}} The pairing usually fights at strawweight, but due to the short notice of the booking, they met at a weight class above.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/05/ufc-on-espn-24-marina-rodriguez-vs-michelle-waterson-strawweight-main-event-official|title=Dana White: Marina Rodriguez vs. Michelle Waterson headlines Saturday's UFC on ESPN 24|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2021-05-04|access-date=2021-05-04}}\n\nA women's bantamweight bout between former [[UFC Women's Bantamweight Championship|UFC Women's Bantamweight Champion]] [[Holly Holm]] and [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Rousey vs. Team Tate]] bantamweight winner [[Julianna Peña]] was scheduled for the event.{{cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/30931675/holly-holm-fight-julianna-pena-ufc-fight-night-8|title=Holly Holm to fight Julianna Pena at UFC Fight Night on May Pena bout, set for surgery|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2021-03-09|access-date=2021-03-09}} In turn, Peña was also removed from the card in favor of a title bout against current champion [[Amanda Nunes]] (also the [[UFC Women's Featherweight Championship|UFC Women's Featherweight Champion]]) at [[UFC 265]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/04/ufc-265-news-amanda-nunes-vs-julianna-pena-august-7|title=Amanda Nunes moves back to bantamweight, defends title vs. Julianna Pena at UFC 265|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2021-04-15|access-date=2021-04-16}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between [[Ben Rothwell]] and [[Philipe Lins]] was expected to take place at [[UFC Fight Night: Edwards vs. Muhammad]], but it was canceled due to an injury to Rothwell.{{cite withdrew from the bout due to an illness.{{cite web|url= https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/05/ufc-on-espn-24-philipe-lins-withdraws-fight-illness-ben-rothwell|title= Philipe Lins out of UFC on ESPN produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on August 7, 2021, at the [[Toyota Center]] in [[Houston]], [[Texas]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/06/ufc-265-news-derrick-lewis-vs-ciryl-gane-interim-title-francis-ngannou|title=UFC 265 heads to Houston with Derrick Lewis vs. Ciryl Gane interim heavyweight championship headliner|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2021-06-28|access-date=2021-06-28}}\n\n==Background==\nAn interim [[UFC Heavyweight Championship]] bout between former title challenger [[Derrick Lewis]] and [[Ciryl Gane]] headlined the event. The original plan was to feature current champion [[Francis Ngannou]] defending his title against Lewis, but the promotion opted to move forward with the interim contest due to the alleged refusal of the champion to defend it in August (despite winning the championship just over four months earlier and claiming he was available for his first defense in September).{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-265-news-francis-ngannou-reaction-derrick-lewis-vs-ciryl-gane-interim-title-fight|title=Francis Ngannou reacts to Lewis vs. Gane interim title booking at UFC 265: 'Nothing has been right'|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2021-06-28|access-date=2021-06-28}}\n\nA [[UFC Women's Bantamweight Championship]] bout between the current champion [[Amanda Nunes]] (also the current [[UFC Women's Featherweight Championship|UFC Women's Featherweight Champion]]) and [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Rousey vs. Team Tate]] bantamweight winner [[Julianna Peña]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/04/ufc-265-news-amanda-nunes-vs-julianna-pena-august-7|title=Amanda Nunes moves back to bantamweight, defends title vs. Julianna Pena at UFC 265|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2021-04-15|access-date=2021-04-16}} However on July 29, the bout was pulled due to Nunes [[COVID-19 testing|testing positive for COVID-19]]. The bout was rescheduled for [[UFC 269]].{{Cite web|url= https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/31912707/bantamweight-champion-amanda-nunes-tests-positive-covid-19-august-title-bout-off|title= Amanda Nunes tests positive for COVID-19; Bantamweight title defense off vs. Julianna Pena|work=espn.com|author=Mike Coppinger|date=2021-07-29|access-date=2021-07-29}}\n\n[[Johnny Muñoz Jr.]] and Jamey Simmons were expected to meet in a bantamweight bout at [[UFC 261]], but the latter pulled out due to undisclosed reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://btgpodcast.co/breaking-johnny-munoz-vs-jamey-simmons-off-for-ufc-261/|title=Johnny Munoz vs Jamey Simmons off for UFC 261|publisher=behindthegrind.co|author=Jack Vegas 32''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on July 24, 2021, at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada|Enterprise]], Nevada, part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/05/ufc-news-tj-dillashaws-return-vs-cory-sandhagen-rebooked-fight-night-july-24|title=T.J. Dillashaw's return vs. Cory Sandhagen rebooked for UFC Fight Night on July 24|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn and Farah Hannoun|date=2021-05-18|access-date=2021-05-18}}\n\n==Background==\n51 VIP premium packages were sold at $2000 each to a selected number of people who could attend the event. This was the first time fans were allowed to attend UFC events at the [[UFC Apex]]. These VIP packages were sold for subsequent events at the venue as well.\n\nA bantamweight bout between former two-time [[UFC Bantamweight Championship|UFC Bantamweight Champion]] [[T.J. Dillashaw]] and [[Cory Sandhagen]] served as the event headliner. It was Dillashaw's first fight since his two-year suspension due to testing positive for [[recombinant human erythropoietin]] (EPO), which resulted in his title vacancy.{{cite web|url=http://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/26313639|title=Dillashaw gives up belt, suspended by NYSAC|publisher=espn.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2019-03-20|access-date=2021-03-19}}{{cite web|url=http://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/26479192/usada-suspends-dillashaw-2-years-epo-use|title=USADA suspends Dillashaw 2 years for EPO use|publisher=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2019-04-09|access-date=2021-03-19}} They were expected to serve as the headliner of [[UFC on ESPN: Rodriguez vs. Waterson]], but the bout was postponed after Dillashaw got injured due to a cut sustained from a headbutt.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/04/ufc-news-tj-dillashaw-facial-cut-out-ufc-main-event-cory-sandhagen|title=Facial cut forces T.J. Dillashaw from UFC comeback fight vs. Cory Sandhagen|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2021-04-27|access-date=2021-04-28}}\n\n[[Raphael Assunção]] and [[Kyler Phillips]] were scheduled to meet in a bantamweight bout.{{cite web|url=https://ge.globo.com/combate/noticia/kyler-phillips-anuncia-luta-com-raphael-assuncao-em-ufc-no-dia-24-de-julho.ghtml|title=Kyler Phillips announces fight with Raphael Assunção in UFC on July 24th|publisher=ge.globo.com|author=Staff|date=2021-04-27|access-date=2021-06-26}} {{in lang|pt}} However, Assunção suffered a biceps injury in late June and was replaced by [[Raulian Paiva]].{{cite web|url=https://ge.globo.com/combate/noticia/raulian-paiva-substitui-raphael-assuncao-e-vai-encarar-kyler-phillips-em-julho.ghtml|title=Raulian Paiva United States.{{Cite web|url=https://www.ufc.com/news/max-holloway-vs-yair-rodriguez-set-ufc-fight-night-july-17|title=Max Holloway vs. Yair Rodriguez set for UFC Fight Night on July 17|publisher=ufc.com|author=Staff|date=2021-05-27|access-date=2021-05-27}}\n\n==Background==\nA featherweight bout between former [[UFC Featherweight Champion]] [[Max Holloway]] and [[The Ultimate Fighter: Latin America]] featherweight winner [[Yair Rodríguez]] was slated to serve as the event headliner.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/05/max-holloway-vs-yair-rodriguez-main-event-ufc-july-17-mma-fight-booking-matchup|title=Max Holloway vs. Yair Rodriguez headlines UFC's July 17 event|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2021-05-26|access-date=2021-05-27}} However, Holloway was pulled from the bout due to injury.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/06/max-holloway-vs-yair-rodriguez-fight-postponed-ufc-on-espn-26-injury|title=Max Holloway out at UFC on ESPN 26, Yair Rodriguez fight postponed|work=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|date=June 17, 2021|access-date=June 17, 2021}} Subsequently, a lightweight bout between [[Islam Makhachev]] and [[Thiago Moisés]] was then promoted to serve as the new main event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/06/ufc-fight-night-islam-makhachev-vs-thiago-moises-main-event-july-17|title=Islam Makhachev vs. Thiago Moises elevated to UFC on Championship|Strikeforce]] and [[UFC Women's Bantamweight Championship|UFC Women's Bantamweight Champion]] [[Miesha Tate]] and [[Marion Reneau]] took place at this event. It was Tate's first fight since [[UFC 205]], where she lost to former title challenger [[Raquel Pennington]] via unanimous decision and decided to retire afterwards.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/03/ufc-miesha-tate-ends-retirement-books-return-marion-reneau-fight-night|title=Miesha Tate returns: Former UFC champ ends retirement, meets Marion Reneau lightweight bout at [[UFC Fight Night: Edwards vs. Muhammad]] four months earlier, but Kutateladze pulled out due to a knee injury that required surgery.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/02/guram-kutateladze-out-march-13-vs-don-madge-knee-injury-ufc|title=Guram Kutateladze says he's out of March 13 UFC fight vs. Don Madge due to knee from the bout citing injury and visa issues.{{Cite web|url= https://www.essentiallysports.com/ufc-mma-news-guram-kutateladze-shares-a-message-after-his-canceled-bout-at-ufc-fight-night/|title=Guram https://www.bjpenn.com/mma-news/ufc/ufc-vegas-31-bonus-report-six-fighters-take-home-an-extra-50k|title=UFC Vegas 31 Bonus Report: Six fighters take home an extra $50k|publisher=bjpenn.com|author=Chris Taylor|date=2021-07-11|access-date=2021-07-11}}\n*'''Fight of the Night: Billy Quarantillo vs. Gabriel Benítez'''\n*'''Performance of the Night: Miesha Tate, Mateusz Gamrot, Rodolfo Vieira,''' and '''Rodrigo Nascimento'''\n\n==Aftermath==\nOn September 22, it was announced that by the [[Nevada State Athletic Commission|Nevada Athletic Commission]] (NAC) that [[Rodrigo Nascimento]] tested positive for [[ritalinic acid]], therefore he received a six-month suspension, retroactive to the test. He was also fined a total of $1,945.36, for the violation and subsequent legal fees. His victory was overturned to a no contest. In late August 2022, it was announced that Nascimento received a [[therapeutic use exemptions|therapeutic use exemption]] (TUE) after proving use of the medication for the treatment of a legitimate medical condition. Since the Nevada commission doesn't accept retroactive TUEs, Nascimento would have to appeal to a higher court to achieve a reinstation of his original result. The three-year TUE went effective on Nov. 12 and is valid for tests conducted by [[USADA]] and NAC. He still must require prior approval from other athletic commissions if he’s scheduled to compete outside of Nevada.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/8/31/23330519/rodrigo-nascimento-granted-tue-ritalin-after-overturned-ufc-vegas-31-win-alan-baudot|title=Rodrigo Nascimento granted TUE for ritalin after overturned UFC Vegas 31 win over Alan Baudot|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2022-08-31|access-date=2022-09-01}}\n\n== See also ==\n{{Portal|Martial Arts}}\n* [[List of UFC event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/juancamilo-ronderos-vs-zarrukh-adashev-toegevoegd-aan-ufc-evenement-op-31-juli/|title=Juancamilo Ronderos vs. Zarrukh Adashev added to UFC event on July web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/07/ufc-news-ryan-benoit-medically-cleared-booked-vs-zarrukh-adashev|title=Ryan Benoit medically cleared for flyweight return; Zarrukh Adashev fight rebooked for UFC on ESPN 27|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and John Morgan|date=2021-07-05|access-date=2021-07-05}} They were originally expected to meet two months earlier at [[UFC on Perez (fighter)|Alex Perez]] and [[Askar Askarov]] was scheduled for the event.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/06/ufc-fight-night-askar-askarov-vs-alex-perez-booked-july-31|title=Askar Askarov vs. Alex Perez added to UFC Fight Night on July web|url=https://espndeportes.espn.com/artes-marciales-mixtas/nota/_/id/8880873/askar-askarov-alex-perez-matt-schnell-brandon-moreno-ufc|title=Askar Askarov breaks his hand and will miss his fight to meet at [[UFC 262]], before Perez was forced to pull out due to undisclosed reasons.{{Cite 262|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2021-04-21|access-date=2021-04-22}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between Ronnie Lawrence and [[John Castañeda]] was scheduled for this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/ronnie-lawrence-vs-john-castaneda-toegevoegd-aan-ufc-on-espn-27-in-las-vegas/|title=Ronnie Lawrence vs. John Castaneda added to UFC on ESPN 28 in Las Vegas|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2021-07-16|access-date=2021-07-19}} {{in lang|nl}} However on July 18, Castaneda pulled out of the bout and was replaced by [[Trevin Jones]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/07/ufc-on-espn-28-trevin-jones-vs-ronnie-lawrence|title=With opponents out, Trevin Jones and Ronnie Lawrence meet at UFC on ESPN 28|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and Farah Hannoun|date=2021-07-18|access-date=2021-07-19}} In turn, the bout was canceled when Lawrence failed to show up for the weigh-ins due to health concerns.{{cite web|url= https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/7/30/22602272/manager-ronnie-lawrences-body-shut-down-after-weight-cut-fight-cancelled-at-ufc-vegas-33|title= Manager: Ronnie Lee|date=2021-07-19|access-date=2021-07-19}}{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/kai-kamaka-iii-vervangt-doo-ho-choi-tegen-danny-chavez-tijdens-ufc-evenement-op-31-juli/|title=Kai Kamaka III replaces Doo Ho Choi against Danny Chavez at UFC July 31 event|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2021-07-19|access-date=2021-07-19}} {{in lang|nl}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between [[Shamil Abdurakhimov]] and [[Chris Daukaus]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|date=2021-07-19|title=Chris Daukaus vs. Shamil Abdurakhimov pulled from UFC Vegas 32, fight now expected to take place on July 31|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/7/19/22583759/chris-daukaus-vs-shamil-abdurakhimov-pulled-from-ufc-vegas-32-fight-expected-take-place-on-july-31|author=Damon Martin|access-date=2021-07-19|publisher=mmafighting.com}} The pairing was initially scheduled to meet one week prior at [[UFC on ESPN: Sandhagen vs. Dillashaw]], but the matchup was removed from that card on July 19 due to [[COVID-19]] protocols within Abdurakhimov's camp. Subsequently, on July 26 the bout was yet again postponed to a future event for unknown reasons.{{Cite web|title=Chris Daukaus vs. Shamil Abdurakhimov scratched from UFC Vegas 33|url=https://mymmanews.com/chris-daukaus-vs-shamil-abdurakhimov-scratched-from-ufc-vegas-33/|publisher=mymmanews.com|author=Adam Martin|date=2021-07-26|access-date=2021-07-26}}\n\n[[Roman Kopylov]] was scheduled to meet [[Sam Alvey]] in a middleweight bout at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/sam-alvey-vs-roman-kopylov-toegevoegd-aan-ufc-evenement-op-31-juli/|title=Sam Alvey vs. Roman and the bout was scrapped.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/7/26/22595222/sam-alvey-vs-roman-kopylov-co-main-event-scrapped-from-ufc-vegas-33-card-on-saturday|title=Sam Alvey vs. Roman report of cancellation|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2021-07-31|access-date=2021-07-31}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, former [[UFC Women's Flyweight Championship|UFC Women's Flyweight Champion]] [[Nicco Montaño]] (also [[The Ultimate Fighter: A New World Champion]] flyweight winner) and [[Philip Rowe]] missed weight for their respective bouts. Montaño weighed in at 143 pounds, seven pounds over the women's bantamweight non-title fight limit. Her bout against [[Wu Yanan (fighter)|Wu Yanan]] was canceled due to the weight discrepancy. Rowe weighed in at 173.5 pounds, two and half pounds over the welterweight non-title fight limit. His bout proceeded at a catchweight and he was fined 20% of his individual purse, which went to his opponent Orion Cosce.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/7/30/22602199/ufc-vegas-33-weigh-in-results-ex-ufc-champion-nicco-montano-misses-by-7-pounds|title=UFC Vegas 33 weigh-in results: Ex-UFC champion Nicco Montano misses by 7 pounds, fight cancelled|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2021-07-30|access-date=2021-07-30}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between [[Kang Kyung-ho]] and [[Rani Yahya]] was expected to serve as the co-main event.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/05/ufc-news-kyung-ho-kang-vs-rani-yahya-dooho-choi-vs-danny-chavez-july-31|title=Kyung Ho Kang vs. Rani Yahya, Dooho Choi vs. Danny Chavez added to UFC event on July 31|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2021-05-24|access-date=2021-07-31}} However, the bout was canceled a few hours before the event due to Yahya [[COVID-19 testing|testing positive for COVID-19]].{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/07/ufc-on-espn-28-rani-yahya-covid-positive-co-main-event-canceled|title=UFC on
